# Graph Report - MASS-L3-Tactical Layer  (2026-05-22)

## Corpus Check
- 1377 files · ~1,595,194 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 24675 nodes · 26897 edges · 2722 communities (2430 shown, 292 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 934 edges (avg confidence: 0.76)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `40b6a4e0`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
